I can't confirm that what I fixed is exactly what the recording shows.\n\n## Defects fixed\n\n**1. Rotated mesh nodes, `src/client/mesh.cpp:271`**\n`rotateMesh` converted the angle with `radToDeg` instead of `degToRad`. Every mesh-drawtype node turned by facedir, wallmounted or degrotate was drawn at a wildly wrong angle. That includes the rotated copies the mesh cache builds when node definitions load.\n\n**2. Connected nodeboxes (fences, panes, walls), `src/client/content_mapblock.cpp`, `nodebox_connection_dirs`**\n\"Front\" and \"back\" were swapped, with front set to +Z. The connection bits and the rest of the engine (`MapNode::getNeighbors`, the `connect_front`/`connect_back` API) treat front as −Z. So a connected node's arms on the Z axis appeared on the opposite side from the neighbour they connect to.\n\n**3. Nodeboxes with `visual_scale ≠ 1`, `drawAutoLightedCuboid` in `content_mapblock.cpp`**\nThe node's position within its map chunk was added before the box was scaled, so the position got scaled along with the size. The node drifted away from its real spot, by an amount that depends on where it sits, which fits \"anywhere in the world\". I moved the position offset to after the scaling.\n\n**Related fix, `NodeDefManager::nodeboxConnects` in `src/nodedef.cpp`**\nThe facedir-rotation lookup had been moved outside the `connect_sides > 0` check. As a result, connected nodes never joined a facedir-rotated neighbour that doesn't restrict its connecting sides. I put the lookup back inside that check.\n\n## What I checked and found correct\n- **Facedir rotations:** a script compared the nodebox rotation, the mesh rotation and the texture lookup table (which tile shows on each face, and how it's turned). All three agree for all 24 facedirs × 6 faces.\n- **Other drawtypes, by hand:** torch, sign, plantlike (including wall-mounted, rooted, degrotate and random offset), firelike, raillike, framed glass, wallmounted nodeboxes, the `makeFastFace` path for normal-drawtype nodes, and client placement prediction (`game.cpp`). Nodeboxes with `visual_scale ≠ 1`, `drawAutoLightedCuboid` in `content_mapblock.cpp`**\nThe node's position within its map chunk was added before the box was scaled, so the position got scaled along with the size. The node drifted away from its real spot, by an amount that depends on where it sits, which fits \"anywhere in the world\". I moved the position offset to after the scaling.\n\n**Related fix, `NodeDefManager::nodeboxConnects` in `src/nodedef.cpp`**\nThe facedir-rotation lookup had been moved outside the `connect_sides > 0` check. As a result, connected nodes never joined a facedir-rotated neighbour that doesn't restrict its connecting sides.
